	var addCurrentLocationAsNextParameter = function (link) {
		var currentLocation = window.location.href;
		if (currentLocation.indexOf('/webflow') > -1) {
			currentLocation += '&_eventId_restartFlow';
		}
		if (link.indexOf('?') > -1) {
			link += '&';
		} else {
			link += '?';
		}
		return link += 'next=' + encodeURIComponent(currentLocation);
	};

	dmSettings.oauth.loginUrl = addCurrentLocationAsNextParameter("/login/468748.html");

	$(document).ready(function () {
		if ($.cookie('dmconsent') !== undefined && true ) {
			if(window.ComponentLogin.hasAccessToken()) {
				var props = {
					title: 'Bitte akzeptieren Sie die neuen Bedingungen, um Mein dm nutzen zu können',
					contentId: '1182362',
					servicesHost: 'https://www.dm.de/content-service',
					tenant: 'de_mcr',
					buttons: ({
						close: {
							handler: function() {
								$.removeCookie("dmconsent", {path: '/'});
								window.location.href = '/cms/logout?targetUrl=&mandantId=100';
							}
						},
						footer2: {
							label: 'Akzeptieren',
							handler: function() {
								var cookie = JSON.parse($.cookie('dmconsent'));
								var tenant = 'de_mcr';
								var path = dmSettings.targetSystem == 'preview' ? ('/cms/servlet/userprofile/' + tenant) : '/userprofile/';
								var formData = new FormData();
								formData.append("condition", cookie.condition);
								formData.append("version", cookie.highestVersion);
								formData.append("_CSRFToken", $.cookie('dmctc'));
								$.ajax({
									url: path,
									type: "POST",
									data: formData,
									processData: false,
									contentType: false,
									success: function() {
										$.removeCookie("dmconsent", {path: '/'});
									}
								});
							}
						},
					}),
					openOnMount: true
				};
                ReactDOM.render(React.createElement(window.DmBaseLayer.default, props), document.getElementById('layer-updateTerms-container'));
			} else {
				/* edge case: if user was logged out but didn't act (e.g. timeout) the cookie should also be removed.
				 Otherwise a not-logged-in user would be promted as well */
				$.removeCookie("dmconsent", {path: '/'});
			}
		}
	});

<script type="text/javascript">
	function loadScript(src, async, onload) {
		var s = document.createElement('script');
		s.type = 'text/javascript';
		s.async = async;
		if (onload) {
			s.onload=onload
		}
		s.src = src;
		var x = document.getElementsByTagName('script')[0];
		x.parentNode.insertBefore(s, x);
	}

	function initUvpBanner() {
        
			var props = {};
			ReactInit.initReactComponent('om-components', 'UvpBannerContainer', props, document.querySelector('[data-dmid="uvpBanner-container"]'));
		
	}

    function initScripts() {
        initNavigation();
        initUvpBanner();
    }

	function onLoadScriptsLoaded() {
		var superView = responsiveView;
		if (typeof shopView != "undefined") {
			superView = shopView;
		}
		if (typeof vtsView !== "undefined") {
			currentView = new vtsView();
		} else {
			currentView = new superView();
		}
        initScripts();

		// this is a very sad hack, because jQuery filter stay in product-ui
		// as soon as we have removed jQuery and this sad backbone resize mechanism
		// we can remove this one here
		if (dmUi["product-view"]) {
			dmUi["product-view"].LegacyDmFilter.initFilter();
		}
    }
	function lazyLoadJs(){
	    // we currently only lazyload on product pages, startpage and storefinder
		// because several content pages and flexchannels failed with lazyloaded scripts
        if (false) {

        	if (true) {
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/dm.faster.than.main.min.js', true, onLoadScriptsLoaded);
			} else {
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/fx.jquery.purl.js', true);
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/dm.jquery.console.logger.js', true);
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/fx.jqueryui-multiOpenAccordion.js', true);
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/fx.jquery.form.js', true);
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/fx.jquery.validate.js', true);
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/fx.smartjax.1.0.js', true);

				<!-- ### Page Default -->
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/fx.jquery.qtip.min.js', true);
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/fx.backbone.js', true);
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/fx.backbone.getter.setter.js', true);
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/fx.underscore.string.js', true);

				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/dm.security.js', true);
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/dm.helper.js', true);

				<!-- ### Components -->
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/fx.swiper2.6.1.js', true);
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/fx.swiper.scrollbar-2.4.1.js', true);
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/fx.swiper3.jquery.js', true);
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/dm.plugins.js', true);

				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/dm.plugins.videoHandler.js', true);
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/dm.plugins.imageGallery.js', true);
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/dm.plugins.tileWall.js', true);
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/dm.plugins.slider.js', true);
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/dm.plugins.stage.js', true);

				<!-- ### Responsive Part -->
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/dm.component.login.js', true);
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/dm.responsive.model.js', true);
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/dm.responsive.view.js', true);

				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/fx.picturePolyfill.js', true);

				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/dm.responsive.modules.js', true);

				<!-- ### UI Layer -->
				loadScript('/cms/3-153-3/scripts/ui-layer/dm.ui-layer.js', true);

				setTimeout(function () {
					onLoadScriptsLoaded()
				}, 1000);
			}

            if ("vts" === 'pds') {
                loadScript('https://services.dm.de/shoppingview/3.56.0/components/shoppingView.min.js', true);
            }
        } else {
            initScripts();
        }
	}
	$(document).ready(function () {
		lazyLoadJs();
	})
</script>
